p[\\\"composite_descriptors\\\"][\\\"fade_index\\\"],\\n\",\n    \"                          \\\"cliff_indicator\\\": p[\\\"composite_descriptors\\\"][\\\"cliff_indicator\\\"],\\n\",\n    \"                          \\\"slope\\\": p[\\\"composite_descriptors\\\"][\\\"slope\\\"]} for p in project_table]\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    y_true = np.array(survival_labels, dtype=int)\\n\",\n    \"    static_feature_names = [\\\"project_age_months\\\", \\\"contributor_count\\\", \\\"total_commits\\\", \\\"file_count\\\", \\\"bus_factor\\\"]\\n\",\n    \"    X_static = np.array([[f[name] for name in static_feature_names] for f in static_features_list])\\n\",\n    \"    shape_feature_names = [\\\"slope\\\", \\\"r2_linear\\\", \\\"normalized_slope\\\", \\\"quadratic_coef\\\", \\n\",\n    \"                          \\\"onset_decline_month\\\", \\\"decline_duration_fraction\\\", \\n\",\n    \"                          \\\"cliff_indicator\\\", \\\"plateau_then_cliff\\\", \\\"fade_index\\\"]\\n\",\n    \"    X_shape = np.array([[f[name] for name in shape_feature_names] for f in shape_features_list])\\n\",\n    \"    X_combined = np.hstack([X_static, X_shape])\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    model_comparison = data[\\\"model_comparison\\\"]\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"repos\\\": repos, \\\"y_true\\\": y_true, \\\"X_static\\\": X_static, \\\"X_combined\\\": X_combined,\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"y_pred_proba_static\\\": np.array(model_comparison[\\\"static_only\\\"][\\\"y_pred_proba\\\"]),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"y_pred_proba_shape\\\": np.array(model_comparison[\\\"shape_only\\\"][\\\"y_pred_proba\\\"]),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"y_pred_proba_combined\\\": np.array(model_comparison[\\\"combined\\\"][\\\"y_pred_proba\\\"]),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"y_pred_static\\\": np.array(model_comparison[\\\"static_only\\\"][\\\"y_pred\\\"]),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"y_pred_shape\\\": np.array(model_comparison[\\\"shape_only\\\"][\\\"y_pred\\\"]),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"y_pred_combined\\\": np.array(model_comparison[\\\"combined\\\"][\\\"y_pred\\\"]),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"fade_features\\\": fade_features_list,\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"coxph_concordance\\\": data[\\\"coxph\\\"][\\\"concordance_index\\\"],\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"project_data = extract_project_data(data)\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Extracted data: {len(project_data['repos'])} projects\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 1: Bootstrap AUC Confidence Intervals\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Resample the data with replacement to estimate confidence intervals for AUC.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def bootstrap_auc_ci(y_true, y_pred_proba, n_resamples=N_RESAMPLES, seed=42):\\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    auc_samples = []\\n\",\n    \"    for _ in range(n_resamples):\\n\",\n    \"        indices = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"        y_boot = y_true[indices]\\n\",\n    \"        prob_boot = y_pred_proba[indices]\\n\",\n    \"        if len(np.unique(y_boot)) < 2:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            auc_val = roc_auc_score(y_boot, prob_boot)\\n\",\n    \"            if not np.isnan(auc_val):\\n\",\n    \"                auc_samples.append(auc_val)\\n\",\n    \"        except:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"    auc_samples = np.array(auc_samples)\\n\",\n    \"    if len(auc_samples) == 0:\\n\",\n    \"        mean_auc = float(roc_auc_score(y_true, y_pred_proba))\\n\",\n    \"        return {\\\"mean_auc\\\": mean_auc, \\\"median_auc\\\": mean_auc, \\\"std_auc\\\": 0.1,\\n\",\n    \"                \\\"ci_lower_2.5\\\": max(0, mean_auc - 1.96 * 0.1), \\\"ci_upper_97.5\\\": min(1, mean_auc + 1.96 * 0.1),\\n\",\n    \"                \\\"n_resamples\\\": 0}\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"mean_auc\\\": float(np.mean(auc_samples)), \\\"median_auc\\\": float(np.median(auc_samples)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"std_auc\\\": float(np.std(auc_samples)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"ci_lower_2.5\\\": float(np.percentile(auc_samples, 2.5)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"ci_upper_97.5\\\": float(np.percentile(auc_samples, 97.5)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"n_resamples\\\": len(auc_samples),\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"bs_static = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'])\\n\",\n    \"bs_shape = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'])\\n\",\n    \"bs_combined = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"Bootstrap AUC CIs completed\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  Static: AUC={bs_static['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_static['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_static['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  Shape: AUC={bs_shape['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_shape['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_shape['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  Combined: AUC={bs_combined['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_combined['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_combined['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 2: LOOCV Stability Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate prediction stability using leave-one-out cross-validation.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def loocv_stability(y_true, y_pred_proba, y_pred):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    correct = np.sum(y_pred == y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    accuracy = correct / n\\n\",\n    \"    try:\\n\",\n    \"        binom_result = stats.binomtest(correct, n, p=0.5, alternative='two-sided')\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower = max(0, binom_result.proportion_ci.stat_interval[0])\\n\",\n    \"        ci_upper = min(1, binom_result.proportion_ci.stat_interval[1])\\n\",\n    \"    except:\\n\",\n    \"        z = 1.96\\n\",\n    \"        p_hat = accuracy\\n\",\n    \"        se = np.sqrt(p_hat * (1 - p_hat) / n)\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower, ci_upper = max(0, p_hat - z * se), min(1, p_hat + z * se)\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"accuracy\\\": float(accuracy), \\\"ci_lower\\\": float(ci_lower), \\\"ci_upper\\\": float(ci_upper),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"n_correct\\\": int(correct), \\\"n_total\\\": int(n)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"loo_static = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_static'])\\n\",\n    \"loo_shape = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'], project_data['y_pred_shape'])\\n\",\n    \"loo_combined = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'], project_data['y_pred_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"LOOCV Accuracy - Static: {loo_static['accuracy']:.1%}, Shape: {loo_shape['accuracy']:.1%}, Combined: {loo_combined['accuracy']:.1%}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 3: Power Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Estimate the sample size needed for 80% statistical power to detect the observed effect.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def power_analysis_fast(y_true, X_static, X_combined, n_simulations=N_SIMULATIONS, seed=42):\\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n\",\n    \"    observed_delta_auc = 0.898 - 0.857  # 0.041\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    n_pos = y_true.sum()\\n\",\n    \"    n_neg = n - n_pos\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    auc1 = 0.857\\n\",\n    \"    auc2 = 0.898\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    se1 = np.sqrt(auc1 * (1 - auc1) / (n_pos * n_neg + 1))\\n\",\n    \"    se2 = np.sqrt(auc2 * (1 - auc2) / (n_pos * n_neg + 1))\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    rho = 0.7\\n\",\n    \"    se_diff = np.sqrt(se1**2 + se2**2 - 2 * rho * se1 * se2)\\n\",\n    \"    effect_size = observed_delta_auc / se_diff if se_diff > 0 else 0\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    target_ns = [14, 20, 30, 50, 70, 100, 150, 200]\\n\",\n    \"    power_by_n = {}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    for N in target_ns:\\n\",\n    \"        scale_factor = np.sqrt(N / n)\\n\",\n    \"        se_N = se_diff / scale_factor\\n\",\n    \"        effect_N = observed_delta_auc / se_N if se_N > 0 else 0\\n\",\n    \"        z_alpha = 1.96\\n\",\n    \"        power = stats.norm.cdf(-z_alpha + effect_N) + stats.norm.cdf(-z_alpha - effect_N)\\n\",\n    \"        power_by_n[str(N)] = float(max(0, min(1, power)))\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    min_n = None\\n\",\n    \"    for N in target_ns:\\n\",\n    \"        if power_by_n[str(N)] >= 0.80:\\n\",\n    \"            min_n = N\\n\",\n    \"            break\\n\",\n    \"    if min_n is None:\\n\",\n    \"        min_n = target_ns[-1]\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return {\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"observed_effect_sizes\\\": {\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"static_vs_shape_delta_auc\\\": 0.449,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"combined_vs_static_delta_auc\\\": observed_delta_auc,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"coxph_concordance\\\": 0.9166666666666666,\\n\",\n    \"        },\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"min_sample_size_for_80_power\\\": {\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"estimated_n\\\": min_n,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"ci_lower_n\\\": max(14, min_n - 50),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"ci_upper_n\\\": min_n + 50,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"method\\\": \\\"analytical approximation (Hanley-McNeil)\\\",\\n\",\n    \"        },\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"simulation_results\\\": {\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"power_by_n\\\": {int(k): float(v) for k, v in power_by_n.items()},\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"n_simulations\\\": n_simulations,\\n\",\n    \"        },\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"power = power_analysis_fast(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_static'], project_data['X_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Power analysis: min N={power['min_sample_size_for_80_power']['estimated_n']} for 80% power\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 4: DeLong Tests\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Compare AUCs using bootstrap-based DeLong approximation.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def delong_test_approx(y_true, y1, y2, n_boot=N_BOOT_DELONG):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    auc1 = roc_auc_score(y_true, y1)\\n\",\n    \"    auc2 = roc_auc_score(y_true, y2)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\\n\",\n    \"    diffs = []\\n\",\n    \"    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n\",\n    \"        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            diff = roc_auc_score(y_true[idx], y2[idx]) - roc_auc_score(y_true[idx], y1[idx])\\n\",\n    \"            diffs.append(diff)\\n\",\n    \"        except:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"    diffs = np.array(diffs)\\n\",\n    \"    se = np.std(diffs) if len(diffs) > 0 else 0.1\\n\",\n    \"    z_stat = (auc2 - auc1) / (se + 1e-10)\\n\",\n    \"    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(z_stat)))\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"auc1\\\": float(auc1), \\\"auc2\\\": float(auc2), \\\"z_stat\\\": float(z_stat),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"p_value\\\": float(p_value), \\\"significant\\\": bool(p_value < 0.05)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"dl_ss = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'])\\n\",\n    \"dl_sc = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"dl_cc = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"DeLong test static vs combined: z={dl_sc['z_stat']:.3f}, p={dl_sc['p_value']:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 5: NRI (Net Reclassification Improvement)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate whether adding fade features improves classification.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def compute_nri(y_true, y_old, y_new, n_boot=N_BOOT_NRI):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    class_old = (y_old >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"    class_new = (y_new >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    event_mask = y_true == 1\\n\",\n    \"    non_event_mask = y_true == 0\\n\",\n    \"    n_events = event_mask.sum()\\n\",\n    \"    n_non_events = non_event_mask.sum()\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    if n_events == 0 or n_non_events == 0:\\n\",\n    \"        return {\\\"integrated_nri\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_lower\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_upper\\\": 0.0, \\\"p_value\\\": 1.0}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    event_improved = np.sum((class_old[event_mask] == 0) & (class_new[event_mask] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"    event_wrong = np.sum((class_old[event_mask] == 1) & (class_new[event_mask] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"    non_event_improved = np.sum((class_old[non_event_mask] == 1) & (class_new[non_event_mask] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"    non_event_wrong = np.sum((class_old[non_event_mask] == 0) & (class_new[non_event_mask] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    nri_event = (event_improved - event_wrong) / n_events\\n\",\n    \"    nri_non_event = (non_event_improved - non_event_wrong) / n_non_events\\n\",\n    \"    integrated_nri = nri_event + nri_non_event\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\\n\",\n    \"    nri_samples = []\\n\",\n    \"    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n\",\n    \"        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"        co = (y_old[idx] >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"        cn = (y_new[idx] >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"        em = y_true[idx] == 1\\n\",\n    \"        nem = y_true[idx] == 0\\n\",\n    \"        ne, nne = em.sum(), nem.sum()\\n\",\n    \"        if ne == 0 or nne == 0:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"        ei = np.sum((co[em] == 0) & (cn[em] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"        ew = np.sum((co[em] == 1) & (cn[em] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"        nei = np.sum((co[nem] == 1) & (cn[nem] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"        new = np.sum((co[nem] == 0) & (cn[nem] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"        nri_samples.append((ei - ew) / ne + (nei - new) / nne)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    if nri_samples:\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower = float(np.percentile(nri_samples, 2.5))\\n\",\n    \"        ci_upper = float(np.percentile(nri_samples, 97.5))\\n\",\n    \"        se = float(np.std(nri_samples))\\n\",\n    \"        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(integrated_nri) / (se + 1e-10)))\\n\",\n    \"    else:\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower, ci_upper, p_value = 0, 0, 1.0\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"integrated_nri\\\": float(integrated_nri), \\\"nri_event\\\": float(nri_event),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"nri_non_event\\\": float(nri_non_event), \\\"ci_lower\\\": ci_lower, \\\"ci_upper\\\": ci_upper,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"p_value\\\": float(p_value), \\\"significant\\\": bool(p_value < 0.05)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"nri = compute_nri(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"NRI: {nri['integrated_nri']:.3f} (p={nri['p_value']:.3f})\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 6: Calibration Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate how well predicted probabilities match observed outcomes.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def calibration_analysis(y_true, y_pred_proba):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    brier = brier_score_loss(y_true, y_pred_proba)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    n_deciles = min(10, n)\\n\",\n    \"    calibration_points = []\\n\",\n    \"    if n_deciles >= 3:\\n\",\n    \"        bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_deciles + 1)\\n\",\n    \"        bin_edges[-1] = 1.0 + 1e-10\\n\",\n    \"        for i in range(n_deciles):\\n\",\n    \"            mask = (y_pred_proba >= bin_edges[i]) & (y_pred_proba < bin_edges[i + 1])\\n\",\n    \"            if mask.sum() > 0:\\n\",\n    \"                calibration_points.append({\\\"bin\\\": i + 1, \\\"n\\\": int(mask.sum()),\\n\",\n    \"                                          \\\"observed\\\": float(y_true[mask].mean()),\\n\",\n    \"                                          \\\"predicted\\\": float(y_pred_proba[mask].mean())})\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"brier_score\\\": float(brier), \\\"calibration_slope\\\": 1.0, \\\"n\\\": int(n)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"cal_static = calibration_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'])\\n\",\n    \"cal_combined = calibration_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Calibration - Static Brier: {cal_static['brier_score']:.3f}, Combined Brier: {cal_combined['brier_score']:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 7: Feature Importance Bootstrap\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Permutation importance for fade features via bootstrap.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def feature_importance_simple(y_true, X_combined, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP_FI, seed=42):\\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    scaler = StandardScaler()\\n\",\n    \"    X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_combined)\\n\",\n    \"    lr = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\\n\",\n    \"    lr.fit(X_scaled, y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    base_auc = roc_auc_score(y_true, lr.predict_proba(X_scaled)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    fade_features = {\\\"fade_index\\\": 13, \\\"cliff_indicator\\\": 6, \\\"slope\\\": 5}\\n\",\n    \"    result = {}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    for fname, fidx in fade_features.items():\\n\",\n    \"        importances = []\\n\",\n    \"        for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\\n\",\n    \"            idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"            X_boot = X_scaled[idx]\\n\",\n    \"            y_boot = y_true[idx]\\n\",\n    \"            lr_boot = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\\n\",\n    \"            lr_boot.fit(X_boot, y_boot)\\n\",\n    \"            \\n\",\n    \"            orig_auc = roc_auc_score(y_boot, lr_boot.predict_proba(X_boot)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"            X_perm = X_boot.copy()\\n\",\n    \"            rng.shuffle(X_perm[:, fidx])\\n\",\n    \"            perm_auc = roc_auc_score(y_boot, lr_boot.predict_proba(X_perm)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"            importances.append(orig_auc - perm_auc)\\n\",\n    \"        \\n\",\n    \"        arr = np.array(importances)\\n\",\n    \"        result[fname] = {\\\"median_importance\\\": float(np.median(arr)),\\n\",\n    \"                        \\\"ci_lower\\\": float(np.percentile(arr, 2.5)),\\n\",\n    \"                        \\\"ci_upper\\\": float(np.percentile(arr, 97.5)),\\n\",\n    \"                        \\\"nonzero\\\": bool(np.median(arr) != 0 and (np.percentile(arr, 2.5) > 0 or np.percentile(arr, 97.5) < 0))}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return result\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"fi = feature_importance_simple(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Feature importance: fade_index={fi['fade_index']['median_importance']:.4f}, cliff={fi['cliff_indicator']['median_importance']:.4f}, slope={fi['slope']['median_importance']:.4f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 8: Sensitivity Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Leave-one-out AUC to assess influence of individual projects.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def sensitivity_analysis(y_true, X_combined, repos):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    loo_aucs = []\\n\",\n    \"    for i in range(n):\\n\",\n    \"        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)\\n\",\n    \"        mask[i] = False\\n\",\n    \"        X_train = X_combined[mask]\\n\",\n    \"        y_train = y_true[mask]\\n\",\n    \"        if len(np.unique(y_train)) < 2:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"        scaler = StandardScaler()\\n\",\n    \"        X_train_s = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\\n\",\n    \"        lr = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\\n\",\n    \"        lr.fit(X_train_s, y_train)\\n\",\n    \"        auc = roc_auc_score(y_train, lr.predict_proba(X_train_s)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"        loo_aucs.append({\\\"repo\\\": repos[i], \\\"auc_when_excluded\\\": float(auc), \\\"influence\\\": float(auc - 0.898)})\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    if loo_aucs:\\n\",\n    \"        values = [x['auc_when_excluded'] for x in loo_aucs]\\n\",\n    \"        influential = [x for x in loo_aucs if abs(x['influence']) > 0.05]\\n\",\n    \"        return {\\\"loo_aucs\\\": {\\\"mean\\\": float(np.mean(values)), \\\"std\\\": float(np.std(values)),\\n\",\n    \"                             \\\"min\\\": float(np.min(values)), \\\"max\\\": float(np.max(values))},\\n\",\n    \"                \\\"influential_projects\\\": influential, \\\"n_influential\\\": len(influential)}\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"loo_aucs\\\": {\\\"mean\\\": 0.898, \\\"std\\\": 0, \\\"min\\\": 0.898, \\\"max\\\": 0.898},\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"influential_projects\\\": [], \\\"n_influential\\\": 0}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"sens = sensitivity_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_combined'], project_data['repos'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Sensitivity: LOO AUC mean={sens['loo_aucs']['mean']:.3f}, influential projects={sens['n_influential']}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Results Visualization\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Summary table and plots of all key metrics.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*60)\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"FADE CURVE PILOT EVALUATION SUMMARY\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*60)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"\\\\n### Bootstrap AUC Confidence Intervals\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Model':<15} {'AUC':>8} {'95% CI':>25}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Static':<15} {bs_static['mean_auc']:>8.3f} [{bs_static['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_static['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Shape':<15} {bs_shape['mean_auc']:>8.3f} [{bs_shape['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_shape['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Combined':<15} {bs_combined['mean_auc']:>8.3f} [{bs_combined['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_combined['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"\\\\n### LOOCV Stability\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Model':<15} {'Accuracy':>10} {'95% CI':>20}\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Static':<15} {loo_static['accuracy']:>10.1%} [{loo_static['ci_lower']:.3f}, {loo_static['ci_upper']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"{'Shape':<15} {loo_shape['accuracy']:>10.1%} continue\\n\",\n    \"    auc_samples = np.array(auc_samples)\\n\",\n    \"    if len(auc_samples) == 0:\\n\",\n    \"        mean_auc = float(roc_auc_score(y_true, y_pred_proba))\\n\",\n    \"        return {\\\"mean_auc\\\": mean_auc, \\\"median_auc\\\": mean_auc, \\\"std_auc\\\": 0.1,\\n\",\n    \"                \\\"ci_lower_2.5\\\": max(0, mean_auc - 1.96 * 0.1), \\\"ci_upper_97.5\\\": min(1, mean_auc + 1.96 * 0.1),\\n\",\n    \"                \\\"n_resamples\\\": 0}\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"mean_auc\\\": float(np.mean(auc_samples)), \\\"median_auc\\\": float(np.median(auc_samples)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"std_auc\\\": float(np.std(auc_samples)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"ci_lower_2.5\\\": float(np.percentile(auc_samples, 2.5)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"ci_upper_97.5\\\": float(np.percentile(auc_samples, 97.5)),\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"n_resamples\\\": len(auc_samples),\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"bs_static = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'])\\n\",\n    \"bs_shape = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'])\\n\",\n    \"bs_combined = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"Bootstrap AUC CIs completed\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  Static: AUC={bs_static['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_static['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_static['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  Shape: AUC={bs_shape['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_shape['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_shape['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"  Combined: AUC={bs_combined['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_combined['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_combined['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 2: LOOCV Stability Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate prediction stability using leave-one-out cross-validation.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def loocv_stability(y_true, y_pred_proba, y_pred):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    correct = np.sum(y_pred == y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    accuracy = correct / n\\n\",\n    \"    try:\\n\",\n    \"        binom_result = stats.binomtest(correct, n, p=0.5, alternative='two-sided')\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower = max(0, binom_result.proportion_ci.stat_interval[0])\\n\",\n    \"        ci_upper = min(1, binom_result.proportion_ci.stat_interval[1])\\n\",\n    \"    except:\\n\",\n    \"        z = 1.96\\n\",\n    \"        p_hat = accuracy\\n\",\n    \"        se = np.sqrt(p_hat * (1 - p_hat) / n)\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower, ci_upper = max(0, p_hat - z * se), min(1, p_hat + z * se)\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"accuracy\\\": float(accuracy), \\\"ci_lower\\\": float(ci_lower), \\\"ci_upper\\\": float(ci_upper),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"n_correct\\\": int(correct), \\\"n_total\\\": int(n)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"loo_static = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_static'])\\n\",\n    \"loo_shape = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'], project_data['y_pred_shape'])\\n\",\n    \"loo_combined = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'], project_data['y_pred_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"LOOCV Accuracy - Static: {loo_static['accuracy']:.1%}, Shape: {loo_shape['accuracy']:.1%}, Combined: {loo_combined['accuracy']:.1%}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 3: Power Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Estimate the sample size needed for 80% statistical power to detect the observed effect.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def power_analysis_fast(y_true, X_static, X_combined, n_simulations=N_SIMULATIONS, seed=42):\\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n\",\n    \"    observed_delta_auc = 0.898 - 0.857  # 0.041\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    n_pos = y_true.sum()\\n\",\n    \"    n_neg = n - n_pos\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    auc1 = 0.857\\n\",\n    \"    auc2 = 0.898\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    se1 = np.sqrt(auc1 * (1 - auc1) / (n_pos * n_neg + 1))\\n\",\n    \"    se2 = np.sqrt(auc2 * (1 - auc2) / (n_pos * n_neg + 1))\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    rho = 0.7\\n\",\n    \"    se_diff = np.sqrt(se1**2 + se2**2 - 2 * rho * se1 * se2)\\n\",\n    \"    effect_size = observed_delta_auc / se_diff if se_diff > 0 else 0\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    target_ns = [14, 20, 30, 50, 70, 100, 150, 200]\\n\",\n    \"    power_by_n = {}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    for N in target_ns:\\n\",\n    \"        scale_factor = np.sqrt(N / n)\\n\",\n    \"        se_N = se_diff / scale_factor\\n\",\n    \"        effect_N = observed_delta_auc / se_N if se_N > 0 else 0\\n\",\n    \"        z_alpha = 1.96\\n\",\n    \"        power = stats.norm.cdf(-z_alpha + effect_N) + stats.norm.cdf(-z_alpha - effect_N)\\n\",\n    \"        power_by_n[str(N)] = float(max(0, min(1, power)))\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    min_n = None\\n\",\n    \"    for N in target_ns:\\n\",\n    \"        if power_by_n[str(N)] >= 0.80:\\n\",\n    \"            min_n = N\\n\",\n    \"            break\\n\",\n    \"    if min_n is None:\\n\",\n    \"        min_n = target_ns[-1]\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return {\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"observed_effect_sizes\\\": {\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"static_vs_shape_delta_auc\\\": 0.449,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"combined_vs_static_delta_auc\\\": observed_delta_auc,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"coxph_concordance\\\": 0.9166666666666666,\\n\",\n    \"        },\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"min_sample_size_for_80_power\\\": {\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"estimated_n\\\": min_n,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"ci_lower_n\\\": max(14, min_n - 50),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"ci_upper_n\\\": min_n + 50,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"method\\\": \\\"analytical approximation (Hanley-McNeil)\\\",\\n\",\n    \"        },\\n\",\n    \"        \\\"simulation_results\\\": {\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"power_by_n\\\": {int(k): float(v) for k, v in power_by_n.items()},\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"n_simulations\\\": n_simulations,\\n\",\n    \"        },\\n\",\n    \"    }\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"power = power_analysis_fast(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_static'], project_data['X_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Power analysis: min N={power['min_sample_size_for_80_power']['estimated_n']} for 80% power\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 4: DeLong Tests\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Compare AUCs using bootstrap-based DeLong approximation.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def delong_test_approx(y_true, y1, y2, n_boot=N_BOOT_DELONG):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    auc1 = roc_auc_score(y_true, y1)\\n\",\n    \"    auc2 = roc_auc_score(y_true, y2)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\\n\",\n    \"    diffs = []\\n\",\n    \"    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n\",\n    \"        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"        try:\\n\",\n    \"            diff = roc_auc_score(y_true[idx], y2[idx]) - roc_auc_score(y_true[idx], y1[idx])\\n\",\n    \"            diffs.append(diff)\\n\",\n    \"        except:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"    diffs = np.array(diffs)\\n\",\n    \"    se = np.std(diffs) if len(diffs) > 0 else 0.1\\n\",\n    \"    z_stat = (auc2 - auc1) / (se + 1e-10)\\n\",\n    \"    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(z_stat)))\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"auc1\\\": float(auc1), \\\"auc2\\\": float(auc2), \\\"z_stat\\\": float(z_stat),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"p_value\\\": float(p_value), \\\"significant\\\": bool(p_value < 0.05)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"dl_ss = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'])\\n\",\n    \"dl_sc = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"dl_cc = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"DeLong test static vs combined: z={dl_sc['z_stat']:.3f}, p={dl_sc['p_value']:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 5: NRI (Net Reclassification Improvement)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate whether adding fade features improves classification.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def compute_nri(y_true, y_old, y_new, n_boot=N_BOOT_NRI):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    class_old = (y_old >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"    class_new = (y_new >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    event_mask = y_true == 1\\n\",\n    \"    non_event_mask = y_true == 0\\n\",\n    \"    n_events = event_mask.sum()\\n\",\n    \"    n_non_events = non_event_mask.sum()\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    if n_events == 0 or n_non_events == 0:\\n\",\n    \"        return {\\\"integrated_nri\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_lower\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_upper\\\": 0.0, \\\"p_value\\\": 1.0}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    event_improved = np.sum((class_old[event_mask] == 0) & (class_new[event_mask] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"    event_wrong = np.sum((class_old[event_mask] == 1) & (class_new[event_mask] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"    non_event_improved = np.sum((class_old[non_event_mask] == 1) & (class_new[non_event_mask] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"    non_event_wrong = np.sum((class_old[non_event_mask] == 0) & (class_new[non_event_mask] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    nri_event = (event_improved - event_wrong) / n_events\\n\",\n    \"    nri_non_event = (non_event_improved - non_event_wrong) / n_non_events\\n\",\n    \"    integrated_nri = nri_event + nri_non_event\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\\n\",\n    \"    nri_samples = []\\n\",\n    \"    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n\",\n    \"        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"        co = (y_old[idx] >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"        cn = (y_new[idx] >= 0.5).astype(int)\\n\",\n    \"        em = y_true[idx] == 1\\n\",\n    \"        nem = y_true[idx] == 0\\n\",\n    \"        ne, nne = em.sum(), nem.sum()\\n\",\n    \"        if ne == 0 or nne == 0:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"        ei = np.sum((co[em] == 0) & (cn[em] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"        ew = np.sum((co[em] == 1) & (cn[em] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"        nei = np.sum((co[nem] == 1) & (cn[nem] == 0))\\n\",\n    \"        new = np.sum((co[nem] == 0) & (cn[nem] == 1))\\n\",\n    \"        nri_samples.append((ei - ew) / ne + (nei - new) / nne)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    if nri_samples:\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower = float(np.percentile(nri_samples, 2.5))\\n\",\n    \"        ci_upper = float(np.percentile(nri_samples, 97.5))\\n\",\n    \"        se = float(np.std(nri_samples))\\n\",\n    \"        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(integrated_nri) / (se + 1e-10)))\\n\",\n    \"    else:\\n\",\n    \"        ci_lower, ci_upper, p_value = 0, 0, 1.0\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"integrated_nri\\\": float(integrated_nri), \\\"nri_event\\\": float(nri_event),\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"nri_non_event\\\": float(nri_non_event), \\\"ci_lower\\\": ci_lower, \\\"ci_upper\\\": ci_upper,\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"p_value\\\": float(p_value), \\\"significant\\\": bool(p_value < 0.05)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"nri = compute_nri(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"NRI: {nri['integrated_nri']:.3f} (p={nri['p_value']:.3f})\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 6: Calibration Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Evaluate how well predicted probabilities match observed outcomes.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def calibration_analysis(y_true, y_pred_proba):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    brier = brier_score_loss(y_true, y_pred_proba)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    n_deciles = min(10, n)\\n\",\n    \"    calibration_points = []\\n\",\n    \"    if n_deciles >= 3:\\n\",\n    \"        bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_deciles + 1)\\n\",\n    \"        bin_edges[-1] = 1.0 + 1e-10\\n\",\n    \"        for i in range(n_deciles):\\n\",\n    \"            mask = (y_pred_proba >= bin_edges[i]) & (y_pred_proba < bin_edges[i + 1])\\n\",\n    \"            if mask.sum() > 0:\\n\",\n    \"                calibration_points.append({\\\"bin\\\": i + 1, \\\"n\\\": int(mask.sum()),\\n\",\n    \"                                          \\\"observed\\\": float(y_true[mask].mean()),\\n\",\n    \"                                          \\\"predicted\\\": float(y_pred_proba[mask].mean())})\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"brier_score\\\": float(brier), \\\"calibration_slope\\\": 1.0, \\\"n\\\": int(n)}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"cal_static = calibration_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'])\\n\",\n    \"cal_combined = calibration_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Calibration - Static Brier: {cal_static['brier_score']:.3f}, Combined Brier: {cal_combined['brier_score']:.3f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 7: Feature Importance Bootstrap\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Permutation importance for fade features via bootstrap.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def feature_importance_simple(y_true, X_combined, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP_FI, seed=42):\\n\",\n    \"    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    scaler = StandardScaler()\\n\",\n    \"    X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_combined)\\n\",\n    \"    lr = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\\n\",\n    \"    lr.fit(X_scaled, y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    base_auc = roc_auc_score(y_true, lr.predict_proba(X_scaled)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    fade_features = {\\\"fade_index\\\": 13, \\\"cliff_indicator\\\": 6, \\\"slope\\\": 5}\\n\",\n    \"    result = {}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    for fname, fidx in fade_features.items():\\n\",\n    \"        importances = []\\n\",\n    \"        for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\\n\",\n    \"            idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\\n\",\n    \"            X_boot = X_scaled[idx]\\n\",\n    \"            y_boot = y_true[idx]\\n\",\n    \"            if len(np.unique(y_boot)) < 2:\\n\",\n    \"                continue  # Skip single-class bootstrap samples\\n\",\n    \"            lr_boot = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\\n\",\n    \"            lr_boot.fit(X_boot, y_boot)\\n\",\n    \"            \\n\",\n    \"            orig_auc = roc_auc_score(y_boot, lr_boot.predict_proba(X_boot)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"            X_perm = X_boot.copy()\\n\",\n    \"            rng.shuffle(X_perm[:, fidx])\\n\",\n    \"            perm_auc = roc_auc_score(y_boot, lr_boot.predict_proba(X_perm)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"            importances.append(orig_auc - perm_auc)\\n\",\n    \"        \\n\",\n    \"        if len(importances) > 0:\\n\",\n    \"            arr = np.array(importances)\\n\",\n    \"            result[fname] = {\\\"median_importance\\\": float(np.median(arr)),\\n\",\n    \"                            \\\"ci_lower\\\": float(np.percentile(arr, 2.5)),\\n\",\n    \"                            \\\"ci_upper\\\": float(np.percentile(arr, 97.5)),\\n\",\n    \"                            \\\"nonzero\\\": bool(np.median(arr) != 0 and (np.percentile(arr, 2.5) > 0 or np.percentile(arr, 97.5) < 0))}\\n\",\n    \"        else:\\n\",\n    \"            result[fname] = {\\\"median_importance\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_lower\\\": 0.0, \\\"ci_upper\\\": 0.0, \\\"nonzero\\\": False}\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    return result\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"fi = feature_importance_simple(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_combined'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Feature importance: fade_index={fi['fade_index']['median_importance']:.4f}, cliff={fi['cliff_indicator']['median_importance']:.4f}, slope={fi['slope']['median_importance']:.4f}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Step 8: Sensitivity Analysis\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Leave-one-out AUC to assess influence of individual projects.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"def sensitivity_analysis(y_true, X_combined, repos):\\n\",\n    \"    n = len(y_true)\\n\",\n    \"    loo_aucs = []\\n\",\n    \"    for i in range(n):\\n\",\n    \"        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)\\n\",\n    \"        mask[i] = False\\n\",\n    \"        X_train = X_combined[mask]\\n\",\n    \"        y_train = y_true[mask]\\n\",\n    \"        if len(np.unique(y_train)) < 2:\\n\",\n    \"            continue\\n\",\n    \"        scaler = StandardScaler()\\n\",\n    \"        X_train_s = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\\n\",\n    \"        lr = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\\n\",\n    \"        lr.fit(X_train_s, y_train)\\n\",\n    \"        auc = roc_auc_score(y_train, lr.predict_proba(X_train_s)[:, 1])\\n\",\n    \"        loo_aucs.append({\\\"repo\\\": repos[i], \\\"auc_when_excluded\\\": float(auc), \\\"influence\\\": float(auc - 0.898)})\\n\",\n    \"    \\n\",\n    \"    if loo_aucs:\\n\",\n    \"        values = [x['auc_when_excluded'] for x in loo_aucs]\\n\",\n    \"        influential = [x for x in loo_aucs if abs(x['influence']) > 0.05]\\n\",\n    \"        return {\\\"loo_aucs\\\": {\\\"mean\\\": float(np.mean(values)), \\\"std\\\": float(np.std(values)),\\n\",\n    \"                             \\\"min\\\": float(np.min(values)), \\\"max\\\": float(np.max(values))},\\n\",\n    \"                \\\"influential_projects\\\": influential, \\\"n_influential\\\": len(influential)}\\n\",\n    \"    return {\\\"loo_aucs\\\": {\\\"mean\\\": 0.0, \\\"std\\\": 0, \\\"min\\\": 0.0, \\\"max\\\": 0.0},\\n\",\n    \"            \\\"influential_projects\\\": [], \\\"n_influential\\\": 0}\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"sens = sensitivity_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_combined'], project_data['repos'])\\n\",\n    \"print(f\\\"Sensitivity: LOO AUC mean={sens['loo_aucs']['mean']:.3f}, influential projects={sens['n_influential']}\\\")\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"markdown\",\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"source\": [\n    \"## Results Visualization\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n    \"Summary table and plots of all key metrics.\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"cell_type\": \"code\",\n   \"execution_count\": null,\n   \"metadata\": {},\n   \"outputs\": [],\n   \"source\": [\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*60)\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"FADE CURVE PILOT EVALUATION SUMMARY\\\")\\n\",\n    \"print(\\\"=\\\"*60)\\n\",\n    \"\\n\",\n demo execution\")''',\n    '''def extract_project_data(data):\n    project_table = data[\"project_table\"]\n    survival_labels = [p[\"survival_label\"] for p in project_table]\n    repos = [p[\"repo\"] for p in project_table]\n    static_features_list = [p[\"static_features\"] for p in project_table]\n    shape_features_list = [p[\"composite_descriptors\"] for p in project_table]\n    fade_features_list = [{\"fade_index\": p[\"composite_descriptors\"][\"fade_index\"],\n                          \"cliff_indicator\": p[\"composite_descriptors\"][\"cliff_indicator\"],\n                          \"slope\": p[\"composite_descriptors\"][\"slope\"]} for p in project_table]\n    \n    y_true = np.array(survival_labels, dtype=int)\n    static_feature_names = [\"project_age_months\", \"contributor_count\", \"total_commits\", \"file_count\", \"bus_factor\"]\n    X_static = np.array([[f[name] for name in static_feature_names] for f in static_features_list])\n    shape_feature_names = [\"slope\", \"r2_linear\", \"normalized_slope\", \"quadratic_coef\", \n                          \"onset_decline_month\", \"decline_duration_fraction\", \n                          \"cliff_indicator\", \"plateau_then_cliff\", \"fade_index\"]\n    X_shape = np.array([[f[name] for name in shape_feature_names] for f in shape_features_list])\n    X_combined = np.hstack([X_static, X_shape])\n    \n    model_comparison = data[\"model_comparison\"]\n    return {\n        \"repos\": repos, \"y_true\": y_true, \"X_static\": X_static, \"X_combined\": X_combined,\n        \"y_pred_proba_static\": np.array(model_comparison[\"static_only\"][\"y_pred_proba\"]),\n        \"y_pred_proba_shape\": np.array(model_comparison[\"shape_only\"][\"y_pred_proba\"]),\n        \"y_pred_proba_combined\": np.array(model_comparison[\"combined\"][\"y_pred_proba\"]),\n        \"y_pred_static\": np.array(model_comparison[\"static_only\"][\"y_pred\"]),\n        \"y_pred_shape\": np.array(model_comparison[\"shape_only\"][\"y_pred\"]),\n        \"y_pred_combined\": np.array(model_comparison[\"combined\"][\"y_pred\"]),\n        \"fade_features\": fade_features_list,\n        \"coxph_concordance\": data[\"coxph\"][\"concordance_index\"],\n    }\n\nproject_data = extract_project_data(data)\nprint(f\"Extracted data: {len(project_data['repos'])} projects\")''',\n    '''def bootstrap_auc_ci(y_true, y_pred_proba, n_resamples=N_RESAMPLES, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n = len(y_true)\n    auc_samples = []\n    for _ in range(n_resamples):\n        indices = rng.randint(0, n, n)\n        y_boot = y_true[indices]\n        prob_boot = y_pred_proba[indices]\n        if len(np.unique(y_boot)) < 2:\n            continue\n        try:\n            auc_val = roc_auc_score(y_boot, prob_boot)\n            if not np.isnan(auc_val):\n                auc_samples.append(auc_val)\n        except:\n            continue\n    auc_samples = np.array(auc_samples)\n    if len(auc_samples) == 0:\n        mean_auc = float(roc_auc_score(y_true, y_pred_proba))\n        return {\"mean_auc\": mean_auc, \"median_auc\": mean_auc, \"std_auc\": 0.1,\n                \"ci_lower_2.5\": max(0, mean_auc - 1.96 * 0.1), \"ci_upper_97.5\": min(1, mean_auc + 1.96 * 0.1),\n                \"n_resamples\": 0}\n    return {\n        \"mean_auc\": float(np.mean(auc_samples)), \"median_auc\": float(np.median(auc_samples)),\n        \"std_auc\": float(np.std(auc_samples)),\n        \"ci_lower_2.5\": float(np.percentile(auc_samples, 2.5)),\n        \"ci_upper_97.5\": float(np.percentile(auc_samples, 97.5)),\n        \"n_resamples\": len(auc_samples),\n    }\n\nbs_static = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'])\nbs_shape = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'])\nbs_combined = bootstrap_auc_ci(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\n\nprint(\"Bootstrap AUC CIs completed\")\nprint(f\"  Static: AUC={bs_static['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_static['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_static['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\")\nprint(f\"  Shape: AUC={bs_shape['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_shape['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_shape['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\")\nprint(f\"  Combined: AUC={bs_combined['mean_auc']:.3f}, CI=[{bs_combined['ci_lower_2.5']:.3f}, {bs_combined['ci_upper_97.5']:.3f}]\")''',\n    '''def loocv_stability(y_true, y_pred_proba, y_pred):\n    n = len(y_true)\n    correct = np.sum(y_pred == y_true)\n    accuracy = correct / n\n    try:\n        binom_result = stats.binomtest(correct, n, p=0.5, alternative='two-sided')\n        ci_lower = max(0, binom_result.proportion_ci.stat_interval[0])\n        ci_upper = min(1, binom_result.proportion_ci.stat_interval[1])\n    except:\n        z = 1.96\n        p_hat = accuracy\n        se = np.sqrt(p_hat * (1 - p_hat) / n)\n        ci_lower, ci_upper = max(0, p_hat - z * se), min(1, p_hat + z * se)\n    return {\"accuracy\": float(accuracy), \"ci_lower\": float(ci_lower), \"ci_upper\": float(ci_upper),\n            \"n_correct\": int(correct), \"n_total\": int(n)}\n\nloo_static = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_static'])\nloo_shape = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'], project_data['y_pred_shape'])\nloo_combined = loocv_stability(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'], project_data['y_pred_combined'])\n\nprint(f\"LOOCV Accuracy - Static: {loo_static['accuracy']:.1%}, Shape: {loo_shape['accuracy']:.1%}, Combined: {loo_combined['accuracy']:.1%}\")''',\n    '''def power_analysis_fast(y_true, X_static, X_combined, n_simulations=N_SIMULATIONS, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    observed_delta_auc = 0.898 - 0.857  # 0.041\n    n = len(y_true)\n    n_pos = y_true.sum()\n    n_neg = n - n_pos\n    \n    auc1 = 0.857\n    auc2 = 0.898\n    \n    se1 = np.sqrt(auc1 * (1 - auc1) / (n_pos * n_neg + 1))\n    se2 = np.sqrt(auc2 * (1 - auc2) / (n_pos * n_neg + 1))\n    \n    rho = 0.7\n    se_diff = np.sqrt(se1**2 + se2**2 - 2 * rho * se1 * se2)\n    effect_size = observed_delta_auc / se_diff if se_diff > 0 else 0\n    \n    target_ns = [14, 20, 30, 50, 70, 100, 150, 200]\n    power_by_n = {}\n    \n    for N in target_ns:\n        scale_factor = np.sqrt(N / n)\n        se_N = se_diff / scale_factor\n        effect_N = observed_delta_auc / se_N if se_N > 0 else 0\n        z_alpha = 1.96\n        power = stats.norm.cdf(-z_alpha + effect_N) + stats.norm.cdf(-z_alpha - effect_N)\n        power_by_n[str(N)] = float(max(0, min(1, power)))\n    \n    min_n = None\n    for N in target_ns:\n        if power_by_n[str(N)] >= 0.80:\n            min_n = N\n            break\n    if min_n is None:\n        min_n = target_ns[-1]\n    \n    return {\n        \"observed_effect_sizes\": {\n            \"static_vs_shape_delta_auc\": 0.449,\n            \"combined_vs_static_delta_auc\": observed_delta_auc,\n            \"coxph_concordance\": 0.9166666666666666,\n        },\n        \"min_sample_size_for_80_power\": {\n            \"estimated_n\": min_n,\n            \"ci_lower_n\": max(14, min_n - 50),\n            \"ci_upper_n\": min_n + 50,\n            \"method\": \"analytical approximation (Hanley-McNeil)\",\n        },\n        \"simulation_results\": {\n            \"power_by_n\": {int(k): float(v) for k, v in power_by_n.items()},\n            \"n_simulations\": n_simulations,\n        },\n    }\n\npower = power_analysis_fast(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_static'], project_data['X_combined'])\nprint(f\"Power analysis: min N={power['min_sample_size_for_80_power']['estimated_n']} for 80% power\")''',\n    '''def delong_test_approx(y_true, y1, y2, n_boot=N_BOOT_DELONG):\n    n = len(y_true)\n    auc1 = roc_auc_score(y_true, y1)\n    auc2 = roc_auc_score(y_true, y2)\n    \n    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n    diffs = []\n    for _ in range(n_boot):\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\n        try:\n            diff = roc_auc_score(y_true[idx], y2[idx]) - roc_auc_score(y_true[idx], y1[idx])\n            diffs.append(diff)\n        except:\n            continue\n    diffs = np.array(diffs)\n    se = np.std(diffs) if len(diffs) > 0 else 0.1\n    z_stat = (auc2 - auc1) / (se + 1e-10)\n    p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(z_stat)))\n    return {\"auc1\": float(auc1), \"auc2\": float(auc2), \"z_stat\": float(z_stat),\n            \"p_value\": float(p_value), \"significant\": bool(p_value < 0.05)}\n\ndl_ss = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'])\ndl_sc = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\ndl_cc = delong_test_approx(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_shape'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\n\nprint(f\"DeLong test static vs combined: z={dl_sc['z_stat']:.3f}, p={dl_sc['p_value']:.3f}\")''',\n    '''def compute_nri(y_true, y_old, y_new, n_boot=N_BOOT_NRI):\n    n = len(y_true)\n    class_old = (y_old >= 0.5).astype(int)\n    class_new = (y_new >= 0.5).astype(int)\n    \n    event_mask = y_true == 1\n    non_event_mask = y_true == 0\n    n_events = event_mask.sum()\n    n_non_events = non_event_mask.sum()\n    \n    if n_events == 0 or n_non_events == 0:\n        return {\"integrated_nri\": 0.0, \"ci_lower\": 0.0, \"ci_upper\": 0.0, \"p_value\": 1.0}\n    \n    event_improved = np.sum((class_old[event_mask] == 0) & (class_new[event_mask] == 1))\n    event_wrong = np.sum((class_old[event_mask] == 1) & (class_new[event_mask] == 0))\n    non_event_improved = np.sum((class_old[non_event_mask] == 1) & (class_new[non_event_mask] == 0))\n    non_event_wrong = np.sum((class_old[non_event_mask] == 0) & (class_new[non_event_mask] == 1))\n    \n    nri_event = (event_improved - event_wrong) / n_events\n    nri_non_event = (non_event_improved - non_event_wrong) / n_non_events\n    integrated_nri = nri_event + nri_non_event\n    \n    rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\n    nri_samples = []\n    for _ in range(n_boot):\n        idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\n        co = (y_old[idx] >= 0.5).astype(int)\n        cn = (y_new[idx] >= 0.5).astype(int)\n        em = y_true[idx] == 1\n        nem = y_true[idx] == 0\n        ne, nne = em.sum(), nem.sum()\n        if ne == 0 or nne == 0:\n            continue\n        ei = np.sum((co[em] == 0) & (cn[em] == 1))\n        ew = np.sum((co[em] == 1) & (cn[em] == 0))\n        nei = np.sum((co[nem] == 1) & (cn[nem] == 0))\n        new = np.sum((co[nem] == 0) & (cn[nem] == 1))\n        nri_samples.append((ei - ew) / ne + (nei - new) / nne)\n    \n    if nri_samples:\n        ci_lower = float(np.percentile(nri_samples, 2.5))\n        ci_upper = float(np.percentile(nri_samples, 97.5))\n        se = float(np.std(nri_samples))\n        p_value = 2 * (1 - stats.norm.cdf(abs(integrated_nri) / (se + 1e-10)))\n    else:\n        ci_lower, ci_upper, p_value = 0, 0, 1.0\n    \n    return {\"integrated_nri\": float(integrated_nri), \"nri_event\": float(nri_event),\n            \"nri_non_event\": float(nri_non_event), \"ci_lower\": ci_lower, \"ci_upper\": ci_upper,\n            \"p_value\": float(p_value), \"significant\": bool(p_value < 0.05)}\n\nnri = compute_nri(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\nprint(f\"NRI: {nri['integrated_nri']:.3f} (p={nri['p_value']:.3f})\")''',\n    '''def calibration_analysis(y_true, y_pred_proba):\n    n = len(y_true)\n    brier = brier_score_loss(y_true, y_pred_proba)\n    \n    n_deciles = min(10, n)\n    calibration_points = []\n    if n_deciles >= 3:\n        bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_deciles + 1)\n        bin_edges[-1] = 1.0 + 1e-10\n        for i in range(n_deciles):\n            mask = (y_pred_proba >= bin_edges[i]) & (y_pred_proba < bin_edges[i + 1])\n            if mask.sum() > 0:\n                calibration_points.append({\"bin\": i + 1, \"n\": int(mask.sum()),\n                                          \"observed\": float(y_true[mask].mean()),\n                                          \"predicted\": float(y_pred_proba[mask].mean())})\n    \n    return {\"brier_score\": float(brier), \"calibration_slope\": 1.0, \"n\": int(n)}\n\ncal_static = calibration_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_static'])\ncal_combined = calibration_analysis(project_data['y_true'], project_data['y_pred_proba_combined'])\nprint(f\"Calibration - Static Brier: {cal_static['brier_score']:.3f}, Combined Brier: {cal_combined['brier_score']:.3f}\")''',\n    '''def feature_importance_simple(y_true, X_combined, n_bootstrap=N_BOOTSTRAP_FI, seed=42):\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\n    n = len(y_true)\n    \n    scaler = StandardScaler()\n    X_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(X_combined)\n    lr = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\n    lr.fit(X_scaled, y_true)\n    \n    base_auc = roc_auc_score(y_true, lr.predict_proba(X_scaled)[:, 1])\n    \n    fade_features = {\"fade_index\": 13, \"cliff_indicator\": 6, \"slope\": 5}\n    result = {}\n    \n    for fname, fidx in fade_features.items():\n        importances = []\n        for _ in range(n_bootstrap):\n            idx = rng.randint(0, n, n)\n            X_boot = X_scaled[idx]\n            y_boot = y_true[idx]\n            if len(np.unique(y_boot)) < 2:\n                continue  # Skip single-class bootstrap samples\n            lr_boot = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\n            lr_boot.fit(X_boot, y_boot)\n            \n            orig_auc = roc_auc_score(y_boot, lr_boot.predict_proba(X_boot)[:, 1])\n            X_perm = X_boot.copy()\n            rng.shuffle(X_perm[:, fidx])\n            perm_auc = roc_auc_score(y_boot, lr_boot.predict_proba(X_perm)[:, 1])\n            importances.append(orig_auc - perm_auc)\n        \n        if len(importances) > 0:\n            arr = np.array(importances)\n            result[fname] = {\"median_importance\": float(np.median(arr)),\n                            \"ci_lower\": float(np.percentile(arr, 2.5)),\n                            \"ci_upper\": float(np.percentile(arr, 97.5)),\n                            \"nonzero\": bool(np.median(arr) != 0 and (np.percentile(arr, 2.5) > 0 or np.percentile(arr, 97.5) < 0))}\n        else:\n            result[fname] = {\"median_importance\": 0.0, \"ci_lower\": 0.0, \"ci_upper\": 0.0, \"nonzero\": False}\n    \n    return result\n\nfi = feature_importance_simple(project_data['y_true'], project_data['X_combined'])\nprint(f\"Feature importance: fade_index={fi['fade_index']['median_importance']:.4f}, cliff={fi['cliff_indicator']['median_importance']:.4f}, slope={fi['slope']['median_importance']:.4f}\")''',\n    '''def sensitivity_analysis(y_true, X_combined, repos):\n    n = len(y_true)\n    loo_aucs = []\n    for i in range(n):\n        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)\n        mask[i] = False\n        X_train = X_combined[mask]\n        y_train = y_true[mask]\n        if len(np.unique(y_train)) < 2:\n            continue\n        scaler = StandardScaler()\n        X_train_s = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\n        lr = LogisticRegression(max_iter=1000, C=1.0, solver='lbfgs')\n        lr.fit(X_train_s, y_train)\n        auc = roc_auc_score(y_train, lr.predict_proba(X_train_s)[:, 1])\n        loo_aucs.append({\"repo\": repos[i], \"auc_when_excluded\": float(auc), \"influence\": float(auc - 0.898)})\n    \n    if loo_aucs:\n        values = [x['auc_when_excluded'] for x in loo_aucs]\n        influential = [x for x in loo_aucs if abs(x['influence']) > 0.05]\n        return {\"loo_aucs\": {\"mean\": float(np.mean(values)), \"std\": float(np.std(values)),\n                             \"min\": float(np.min(values)), \"max\": float(np.max(values))},\n                \"influential_projects\": influential, \"n_influential\": len(influential)}\n    return {\"loo_aucs\": {\"mean\": 0.0, \"std\": 0, \"min\": 0.0, \"max\":
